# MASTER STORYBOARD — GOTHAM / 60 SCENES

**24 fps · 1920×1080 · procedural design draft, not measured performance.** All dialogue is narrator-read: there is no separate in-scene recorded dialogue track, so every visible mouth stays closed. Scene timings are estimates at 160 words/minute plus 0.25s minimum per shot; final audio must be aligned and retimed. Measurements are design coordinates, not observations. Scene-boundary state is locked per asset in the same set; hard cuts, flashbacks and camera changes use explicit reset/asset handoff, not physical seamlessness. The following 2.5D projection is layered perspective, not true volumetric geometry.

## Shared implementation contract

`safeInterpolate(f,a,b,opt) = interpolate(f,a,b,{...opt,extrapolateLeft:"clamp",extrapolateRight:"clamp"})`; `E=Easing.bezier(0.25,0.1,0.25,1)`; `spring({frame:Math.max(0,frame-f0),fps:24,config:{mass:1,damping:18,stiffness:100}})`. All frame intervals below are inclusive; interpolation windows `[start,end]` clamp at both ends. In a Remotion `Sequence`, `frame=useCurrentFrame()` is local and `global=sceneStart+frame`. End-frame interpolation using `[start,end]` handles one-frame beats by returning final value. 

5-plane setup: CSS `perspective:1200px`, preserve-3d, plane `(Z,zIndex,parallax)` = `(-600,10,.25),(-300,20,.60),(-100,30,.85),(0,40,1),(150,60,1.35)`. World camera `translate3d(-camX*p,-camY*p,z) scale(camZ)` per plane; ground plane is the coordinate reference. Do not apply CSS perspective scale a second time if using baked parallax; select one projection pipeline and calibrate. Props within vehicles: interior 36, clipped lower body 38, upper body 41, window frame 43, exterior and fog 45; foreground 60. Clip coordinates must follow vehicle transforms. The scale `100px=1m` is a design base at ground plane; apparent height changes with camera zoom. Head bounds below are WORLD coordinates, not necessarily screen-space: validate screen-safe bounds after camera projection, zoom, movement, and occlusion. Named 24/35/50/85mm lenses are artistic framing labels, not calibrated physical optics.

**Rig conventions:** rotations local to parent, degrees; anatomical forward elbows negative in [-140,0], knees positive in [0,135]; x/y world pixels, Y increases downward. Node 18 contains both ankles and both toes. `pose` arrays follow the 18-node order below. `blendPoses(a,b,6)` is a design helper blending over six frames; implement it. Feet in stance are pinned to world space using IK; move the pelvis or use foot release for swing, not a blind ankle rotation. Zero sliding is an implementation target requiring render validation. No mathematically literal guarantee of non-static movement in every four-frame window, especially for intentionally locked poses or clamped frames.

**Face channels** list: inner L/R; outer L/R; lower L/R+crease; upper lids L/R; lower squint L/R; blink; gaze X/Y+pupil+catchlight; nostril; cheek/shade; fold; lip corners; tremble; jaw; mouth/viseme. Narration is offscreen so CLOSED_M throughout; if separately recorded character dialogue is added, supply phoneme-aligned visemes only from actual audio. A voiced quotation within narration is not automatically on-screen dialogue.

**Lighting/post:** 5-tone color values are stylized swatches, not physically calibrated skin shaders; vignette/grain values are relative 0–1. `SafeSFX` is a proposed component, not a Remotion built-in. Gain 0.10–0.15 linear on licensed effects; final mix must be measured by ear/meters. All elements below are production targets requiring renderer, assets and audio sync to validate. No image/video/audio has been generated.


# Scene_01 — Batman appears at rain-soaked bus street

**Narration:** The first time you see Batman, you think he has come to save you.

## 1. Timeline and micro-beats
Global frames: 0–131; local frames: 0–131; duration 132 frames (5.500s); 14 words. `frame=useCurrentFrame()` within `<Sequence from={0} durationInFrames={132}>`.

- Beat 001 local 0–11 / global 0–11: anticipate; `q=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,11],[-1,0],{easing:Easing.bezier(.25,.1,.25,1)})`; `s=spring({frame:Math.max(0,frame-0),fps:24,config:{mass:1,damping:18,stiffness:100}})`. Apply q as px body sway; spring is bounded via `Math.min(1,s)` for secondary motion.
- Beat 002 local 12–23 / global 12–23: drive; `q=safeInterpolate(frame,[12,23],[0,3],{easing:Easing.bezier(.25,.1,.25,1)})`; `s=spring({frame:Math.max(0,frame-12),fps:24,config:{mass:1,damping:18,stiffness:100}})`. Apply q as px body sway; spring is bounded via `Math.min(1,s)` for secondary motion.

Apply q as px body sway; spring is bounded via `Math.min(1,s)` for secondary motion.

- Beat 008 local 84–95 / global 84–95: peak; `q=safeInterpolate(frame,[84,95],[3,4],{easing:Easing.bezier(.25,.1,.25,1)})`; `s=spring({frame:Math.max(0,frame-84),fps:24,config:{mass:1,damping:18,stiffness:100}})`. Apply q as px body sway; spring is bounded via `Math.min(1,s)` for secondary motion.
- Beat 009 local 96–107 / global 96–107: recoil; `q=safeInterpolate(frame,[96,107],[4,1],{easing:Easing.bezier(.25,.1,.25,1)})`; `s=spring({frame:Math.max(0,frame-96),fps:24,config:{mass:1,damping:18,stiffness:100}})`. ## 2. World grid and virtual camera
`groundY=860; bus=850×300; cruiser=450×155; counter=75; seat=45` px at base scale. Alex: root start/end `(650,860,0)`; height 170px; head bbox `(628,690,672,734)`. Batman: root start/end `(860,860,0)`; height 188px; head bbox `(838,672,882,716)`. Root separation ≥210px when multiple cast listed; interaction requires an intentional contact offset, not simultaneous root overlap. Lens label 50mm; `camX=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,131],[0,4],{easing:E}); camY=0; camZ=1.0; camRotateZ=0; dofBlur=5px` foreground, 0px subject. Locked camera base; optional bodycam drift `dx=.4*sin(frame*.11),dy=.3*cos(frame*.09)` disabled for continuity-locked cuts.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Alex** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
**Batman** root `(860,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Alex: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.
Batman: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Alex: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.
Batman: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,131],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":1,"localFrame":0,"set":"Batman appears at rain-soaked bus street","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Batman":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":1,"localFrame":131,"set":"Batman appears at rain-soaked bus street","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Batman":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Alex: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Alex: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,221],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":4,"localFrame":0,"set":"Wire on locked bus; Joker watches unseen","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

## 2. World grid and virtual camera
`groundY=860; bus=850×300; cruiser=450×155; counter=75; seat=45` px at base scale. Father: root start/end `(650,860,0)`; height 168px; head bbox `(628,692,672,736)`. Root separation ≥210px when multiple cast listed; interaction requires an intentional contact offset, not simultaneous root overlap. Lens label 50mm; `camX=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,347],[0,4],{easing:E}); camY=0; camZ=1.0; camRotateZ=0; dofBlur=5px` foreground, 0px subject. Locked camera base; optional bodycam drift `dx=.4*sin(frame*.11),dy=.3*cos(frame*.09)` disabled for continuity-locked cuts.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Interior variant: shelves, tools and furniture replace road dressing; use interior clipping where applicable. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Father** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Father: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.45, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Father: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,347],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#E7B97B,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":6,"localFrame":0,"set":"Father locks shop; lamp in window","actors":{"Father":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":6,"localFrame":347,"set":"Father locks shop; lamp in window","actors":{"Father":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_07 — Father explains lamp as marker of home

**Narration:** You once asked him why he bothered. A lamp wasn't going to stop a criminal.  No, he said. But it tells you which window is home.

P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Interior variant: shelves, tools and furniture replace road dressing; use interior clipping where applicable. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Father** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
**Alex** root `(860,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Father: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.45, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.
Alex: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.45, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Father: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.
Alex: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,239],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#E7B97B,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":7,"localFrame":0,"set":"Father explains lamp as marker of home","actors":{"Father":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Alex":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":7,"localFrame":239,"set":"Father explains lamp as marker of home","actors":{"Father":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Alex":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Alex: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.
Mara: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Alex: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.
Mara: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,212],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":10,"localFrame":0,"set":"Mara and Alex at reinforced precinct","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":10,"localFrame":212,"set":"Mara and Alex at reinforced precinct","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_12 — Mara approaches anxious bank employee

**Narration:** Mara walks past them and stops beside a bank employee sitting on the curb. The woman keeps staring at her hands.  Her brother worked inside. No one has told her whether he made it out.

## 2. World grid and virtual camera
`groundY=860; bus=850×300; cruiser=450×155; counter=75; seat=45` px at base scale. Mara: root start/end `(650,860,0)`; height 166px; head bbox `(628,694,672,738)`. Employee: root start/end `(860,860,0)`; height 164px; head bbox `(838,696,882,740)`. Root separation ≥210px when multiple cast listed; interaction requires an intentional contact offset, not simultaneous root overlap. Lens label 50mm; `camX=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,320],[0,4],{easing:E}); camY=0; camZ=1.0; camRotateZ=0; dofBlur=5px` foreground, 0px subject. Locked camera base; optional bodycam drift `dx=.4*sin(frame*.11),dy=.3*cos(frame*.09)` disabled for continuity-locked cuts.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Mara** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
**Employee** root `(860,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Mara: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Employee: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Mara: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.
Employee: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(1.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.5px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,320],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":12,"localFrame":0,"set":"Mara approaches anxious bank employee","actors":{"Mara":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Employee":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":12,"localFrame":320,"set":"Mara approaches anxious bank employee","actors":{"Mara":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Employee":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Alex: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.
Mara: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.
Employee: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(1.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.5px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,230],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":13,"localFrame":0,"set":"Mara gives Alex water bottle","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Employee":{"root":[1070,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":13,"localFrame":230,"set":"Mara gives Alex water bottle","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Employee":{"root":[1070,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

## 2. World grid and virtual camera
`groundY=860; bus=850×300; cruiser=450×155; counter=75; seat=45` px at base scale. Alex: root start/end `(650,860,0)`; height 170px; head bbox `(628,690,672,734)`. Root separation ≥210px when multiple cast listed; interaction requires an intentional contact offset, not simultaneous root overlap. Lens label 35mm; `camX=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,239],[0,4],{easing:E}); camY=0; camZ=1.0; camRotateZ=0; dofBlur=5px` foreground, 0px subject. Locked camera base; optional bodycam drift `dx=.4*sin(frame*.11),dy=.3*cos(frame*.09)` disabled for continuity-locked cuts.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Alex** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Alex: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Alex: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,239],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":16,"localFrame":0,"set":"Bat-Signal illuminates clouds","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":16,"localFrame":239,"set":"Bat-Signal illuminates clouds","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_17 — Mara drives while Alex looks up

**Narration:** You look up. Mara keeps driving.  You ask if Batman will catch the Joker.  Maybe, she says. But even if he does, somebody still has to answer the phones.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,266],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":17,"localFrame":0,"set":"Mara drives while Alex looks up","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":17,"localFrame":266,"set":"Mara drives while Alex looks up","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_18 — Robber hangs from fire escape

**Narration:** On your third night, a robbery turns into a chase through the Narrows. By the time you arrive, the robber is hanging upside down from a fire escape, wrapped in a cable. A stolen bag lies beneath him, untouched.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Alex** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,257],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":20,"localFrame":0,"set":"Paramedics and dangling robber","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Robber":{"root":[1070,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":20,"localFrame":257,"set":"Paramedics and dangling robber","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Robber":{"root":[1070,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_21 — Residents stare at rooftop

**Narration:** She points to the people watching from their windows. They're not watching the robber. They're watching the roof.  In Gotham, she says, even the good news makes people afraid to look up.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Mara: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,293],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

# Scene_22 — Red-ribbon package at precinct

**Narration:** On your fourth day, a package arrives at the station. No return address. A bright red ribbon around a plain white box.  The desk sergeant clears the room and calls the bomb squad.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Interior variant: shelves, tools and furniture replace road dressing; use interior clipping where applicable. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

# Scene_23 — Specialist releases wind-up toy

**Narration:** Inside, they find a cheap wind-up toy. A little policeman with a painted smile.  Its key has been turned all the way. When the specialist releases it, the toy marches across the table, bumps into a wall, and falls over.

## 2. World grid and virtual camera
`groundY=860; bus=850×300; cruiser=450×155; counter=75; seat=45` px at base scale. Specialist: root start/end `(650,860,0)`; height 172px; head bbox `(628,688,672,732)`. Root separation ≥210px when multiple cast listed; interaction requires an intentional contact offset, not simultaneous root overlap. Lens label 85mm; `camX=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,365],[0,4],{easing:E}); camY=0; camZ=1.0; camRotateZ=0; dofBlur=5px` foreground, 0px subject. Locked camera base; optional bodycam drift `dx=.4*sin(frame*.11),dy=.3*cos(frame*.09)` disabled for continuity-locked cuts.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Interior variant: shelves, tools and furniture replace road dressing; use interior clipping where applicable. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Specialist** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Specialist: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.45, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Specialist: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,365],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#E7B97B,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 2. World grid and virtual camera
`groundY=860; bus=850×300; cruiser=450×155; counter=75; seat=45` px at base scale. Specialist: root start/end `(650,860,0)`; height 172px; head bbox `(628,688,672,732)`. Root separation ≥210px when multiple cast listed; interaction requires an intentional contact offset, not simultaneous root overlap. Lens label 85mm; `camX=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,212],[0,4],{easing:E}); camY=0; camZ=1.0; camRotateZ=0; dofBlur=5px` foreground, 0px subject. Locked camera base; optional bodycam drift `dx=.4*sin(frame*.11),dy=.3*cos(frame*.09)` disabled for continuity-locked cuts.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Interior variant: shelves, tools and furniture replace road dressing; use interior clipping where applicable. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Specialist** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Specialist: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.45, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Specialist: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,212],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#E7B97B,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":24,"localFrame":0,"set":"Message attached to toy","actors":{"Specialist":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":24,"localFrame":212,"set":"Message attached to toy","actors":{"Specialist":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_25 — Toy sealed in evidence
