## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Interior variant: shelves, tools and furniture replace road dressing; use interior clipping where applicable. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 2. World grid and virtual camera
`groundY=860; bus=850×300; cruiser=450×155; counter=75; seat=45` px at base scale. Alex: root start/end `(650,860,0)`; height 170px; head bbox `(628,690,672,734)`. Mara: root start/end `(860,860,0)`; height 166px; head bbox `(838,694,882,738)`. Root separation ≥210px when multiple cast listed; interaction requires an intentional contact offset, not simultaneous root overlap. Lens label 35mm; `camX=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,365],[0,4],{easing:E}); camY=0; camZ=1.0; camRotateZ=0; dofBlur=5px` foreground, 0px subject. Locked camera base; optional bodycam drift `dx=.4*sin(frame*.11),dy=.3*cos(frame*.09)` disabled for continuity-locked cuts.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Alex** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
**Mara** root `(860,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Alex: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.
Mara: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Alex: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.
Mara: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,365],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":27,"localFrame":0,"set":"Blackout moves across city","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":27,"localFrame":365,"set":"Blackout moves across city","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_28 — Dispatch calls over cruiser radio

**Narration:** Calls flood the radio. A bridge is jammed. An alarm is ringing at a hospital. Somewhere near Robinson Park, people report a bus stopped in the middle of the street.  You and Mara are closest.

## 2. World grid and virtual camera
`groundY=860; bus=850×300; cruiser=450×155; counter=75; seat=45` px at base scale. Alex: root start/end `(650,860,0)`; height 170px; head bbox `(628,690,672,734)`. Mara: root start/end `(860,860,0)`; height 166px; head bbox `(838,694,882,738)`. Root separation ≥210px when multiple cast listed; interaction requires an intentional contact offset, not simultaneous root overlap. Lens label 50mm; `camX=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,320],[0,4],{easing:E}); camY=0; camZ=1.0; camRotateZ=0; dofBlur=5px` foreground, 0px subject. Locked camera base; optional bodycam drift `dx=.4*sin(frame*.11),dy=.3*cos(frame*.09)` disabled for continuity-locked cuts.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Alex** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
**Mara** root `(860,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Alex: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.
Mara: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Alex: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.
Mara: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,320],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":28,"localFrame":0,"set":"Dispatch calls over cruiser radio","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":28,"localFrame":320,"set":"Dispatch calls over cruiser radio","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

## 2. World grid and virtual camera
`groundY=860; bus=850×300; cruiser=450×155; counter=75; seat=45` px at base scale. Driver: root start/end `(650,860,0)`; height 172px; head bbox `(628,688,672,732)`. Alex: root start/end `(860,860,0)`; height 170px; head bbox `(838,690,882,734)`. Root separation ≥210px when multiple cast listed; interaction requires an intentional contact offset, not simultaneous root overlap. Lens label 85mm; `camX=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,230],[0,4],{easing:E}); camY=0; camZ=1.0; camRotateZ=0; dofBlur=5px` foreground, 0px subject. Locked camera base; optional bodycam drift `dx=.4*sin(frame*.11),dy=.3*cos(frame*.09)` disabled for continuity-locked cuts.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Driver** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
**Alex** root `(860,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Driver: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.
Alex: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Driver: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(1.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.5px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.
Alex: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,230],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":31,"localFrame":0,"set":"Phone rings in driver pocket","actors":{"Driver":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Alex":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":31,"localFrame":230,"set":"Phone rings in driver pocket","actors":{"Driver":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Alex":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

# Scene_33 — Mara answers phone on speaker

**Narration:** The phone keeps ringing.  Mara answers on speaker.  A man's voice asks whether the city is enjoying the dark. You know that voice, even though you have never heard it in person. Every cop in Gotham does.

## 2. World grid and virtual camera
`groundY=860; bus=850×300; cruiser=450×155; counter=75; seat=45` px at base scale. Nia: root start/end `(650,860,0)`; height 115px; head bbox `(628,745,672,789)`. Root separation ≥210px when multiple cast listed; interaction requires an intentional contact offset, not simultaneous root overlap. Lens label 85mm; `camX=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,212],[0,4],{easing:E}); camY=0; camZ=1.0; camRotateZ=0; dofBlur=5px` foreground, 0px subject. Locked camera base; optional bodycam drift `dx=.4*sin(frame*.11),dy=.3*cos(frame*.09)` disabled for continuity-locked cuts.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Nia** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Nia: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Nia: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(1.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.5px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,212],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":35,"localFrame":0,"set":"Nia drops flashlight inside bus","actors":{"Nia":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":35,"localFrame":212,"set":"Nia drops flashlight inside bus","actors":{"Nia":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_36 — Alex steps toward bus door

**Narration:** The bomb squad is on its way, but the roads are blocked. The driver says the girl has been alone for nearly half an hour. The windows are getting harder to see through.  You take a step toward the door.

Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":36,"localFrame":0,"set":"Alex steps toward bus door","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":36,"localFrame":365,"set":"Alex steps toward bus door","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_37 — Batman lands and cape settles

**Narration:** Something lands on the roof above you. A heavy shape. A cape settles against the brick.  Batman drops into the street.  For a second, nobody moves. The driver steps back. Even Mara looks up.

Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

# Scene_38 — Batman takes radio, warns dispatch

**Narration:** You start explaining, but Batman has already seen the wire near the door. He takes your radio.  Don't open it.  Then he tells dispatch what he's found. His voice is low, steady. He asks Mara to move everyone back. You expect him to open the bus himself.  He doesn't.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.

## 2. World grid and virtual camera
`groundY=860; bus=850×300; cruiser=450×155; counter=75; seat=45` px at base scale. Batman: root start/end `(650,860,0)`; height 188px; head bbox `(628,672,672,716)`. Mara: root start/end `(860,860,0)`; height 166px; head bbox `(838,694,882,738)`. Root separation ≥210px when multiple cast listed; interaction requires an intentional contact offset, not simultaneous root overlap. Lens label 50mm; `camX=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,455],[0,4],{easing:E}); camY=0; camZ=1.0; camRotateZ=0; dofBlur=5px` foreground, 0px subject. Locked camera base; optional bodycam drift `dx=.4*sin(frame*.11),dy=.3*cos(frame*.09)` disabled for continuity-locked cuts.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Batman** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
**Mara** root `(860,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Batman: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.
Mara: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Batman: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.
Mara: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,455],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":40,"localFrame":0,"set":"Batman answers second call","actors":{"Batman":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":40,"localFrame":455,"set":"Batman answers second call","actors":{"Batman":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_41 — Batman asks hospital; call ends

**Narration:** Batman asks which hospital.  The Joker laughs. Find out.  The call ends.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Batman** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Batman: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Batman: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,113],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":41,"localFrame":0,"set":"Batman asks hospital; call ends","actors":{"Batman":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":41,"localFrame":113,"set":"Batman asks hospital; call ends","actors":{"Batman":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_42 — Mara receives hospital report

**Narration:** Batman looks at Mara. She has already requested confirmation from dispatch. A moment later, the report comes back. One hospital has lost backup power, and emergency crews are responding. No one yet knows whether the Joker caused it.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,347],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":42,"localFrame":0,"set":"Mara receives hospital report","actors":{"Batman":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":42,"localFrame":347,"set":"Mara receives hospital report","actors":{"Batman":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_43 — Batman assesses bus and points to wire

**Narration:** Batman looks at the child. Then at the far end of the street.  You think he's about to leave her.  He tells Mara the bomb squad is two blocks away. He points out the wire and asks her to relay everything he's seen.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

# Scene_45 — Alex watches roof; Nia at window

## 2. World grid and virtual camera
`groundY=860; bus=850×300; cruiser=450×155; counter=75; seat=45` px at base scale. Alex: root start/end `(650,860,0)`; height 170px; head bbox `(628,690,672,734)`. Nia: root start/end `(860,860,0)`; height 115px; head bbox `(838,745,882,789)`. Root separation ≥210px when multiple cast listed; interaction requires an intentional contact offset, not simultaneous root overlap. Lens label 50mm; `camX=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,428],[0,4],{easing:E}); camY=0; camZ=1.0; camRotateZ=0; dofBlur=5px` foreground, 0px subject. Locked camera base; optional bodycam drift `dx=.4*sin(frame*.11),dy=.3*cos(frame*.09)` disabled for continuity-locked cuts.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Alex** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
**Nia** root `(860,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Alex: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.
Nia: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Alex: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.
Nia: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(1.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.5px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,428],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":45,"localFrame":0,"set":"Alex watches roof; Nia at window","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Nia":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":45,"localFrame":428,"set":"Alex watches roof; Nia at window","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Nia":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_46 — Nia cries; memory of shop lamp

**Narration:** The girl starts crying. You can't hear her through the glass, but you can see her shoulders shaking.  You remember your father's shop. The little lamp in the window.

## 2. World grid and virtual camera
`groundY=860; bus=850×300; cruiser=450×155; counter=75; seat=45` px at base scale. Nia: root start/end `(650,860,0)`; height 115px; head bbox `(628,745,672,789)`. Root separation ≥210px when multiple cast listed; interaction requires an intentional contact offset, not simultaneous root overlap. Lens label 50mm; `camX=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,266],[0,4],{easing:E}); camY=0; camZ=1.0; camRotateZ=0; dofBlur=5px` foreground, 0px subject. Locked camera base; optional bodycam drift `dx=.4*sin(frame*.11),dy=.3*cos(frame*.09)` disabled for continuity-locked cuts.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.
**Nia** root `(650,860,0)`; nodes 1–18: `pelvis=(tilt:0,twist:0,bob:q*.25); lowerSpine=(lean:q*.25,side:0); thorax=(pitch:q*.15,yawPx:0,breathScale:1); neck=(pitch:q*.1,yaw:0); head=(tilt:q*.1,turnX:0,nodY:0); clavL=0px; clavR=0px; shoulderL=-4+q; shoulderR=4-q; elbowL=-25; elbowR=-25; wristL=0; wristR=0; hipL=0; hipR=0; kneeL=5; kneeR=5; ankleL/R=(0,0); toeL/R=(0,0)`. All q from each local micro-beat. Pose-to-pose exact values are generated from q; these are neutral blocking poses, not a fully authored acting performance.
Grounded gait if and only if a walk is specifically staged: heel +18°, flat 0°, push-off -22°, swing +12°; solve stance foot with world-pinned IK and shorten stride if constraints fail. Neutral planted pose otherwise.

## 5. Hands and prop lifecycle
Both hands base per finger `[MCP,PIP,DIP]` degrees: thumb `[10,8,5]`, index `[5,5,3]`, middle `[5,5,3]`, ring `[5,5,3]`, pinky `[5,5,3]`. At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Nia: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Pupil clipped to per-eye scleraMask, eye gaze solved locally. Flashlight pupil response `.75→.28` only when light actually reaches eye, not automatically. Blink lid envelope `[12,13,14]→[.85,0,.85]`.

## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Nia: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(1.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.5px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,266],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":46,"localFrame":0,"set":"Nia cries; memory of shop lamp","actors":{"Nia":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":46,"localFrame":266,"set":"Nia cries; memory of shop lamp","actors":{"Nia":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_47 — Alex signals Nia with old flashlight

**Narration:** You pull out the old flashlight. The beam flickers. You tap the switch, point it toward the bus, and raise your free hand.  The girl sees you. She wipes her face and raises her hand too.

At a scripted hand contact use grip `[35,30,20],[50,55,35],[55,60,40],[55,60,40],[50,55,35]`; lerp on frames 12–18. Socket layers: back palm 38, oriented prop 39, front fingers 40; prop follows wrist matrix. Default lifecycle rest `(960,830,0)` → optional grab at 18 → optional release at 30, ONLY if action in scene requires it; otherwise no artificial grab/drop. For release `t=(frame-30)/24`, `y=y0+vy*t+.5*980*t*t`; clamp at groundY and set persistent settled state; collision and bounce need simulation/asset-specific bounds.

## 6. Facial rig — all 14 channels per visible character
Alex: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. Nia: `innerL/R=.20/.20; outerL/R=.10/.10; browLowerL/R=.15/.15,crease=.10; upperL/R=.85/.85; lowerL/R=.10/.10; blink=[12..14]` if within scene; `gaze=(960,650), pupil=0.75, catchlight=(+2,-2)`; nostril=.10; cheek=.10,shade=.08; fold=.10; lipCorners=(0,0); tremble=`sin(frame*1.9)*.1px`; jaw=.10; mouth=`CLOSED_M`, speaking=false throughout. ## 7. Breathing and autonomic life
Alex: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(0.45/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.1px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.
Nia: `thoraxRiseY=sin(frame*2*PI*(1.25/24))*1.2`; hand tremor `sin(frame*1.9)*0.5px`. Tears only if visible and scripted; proposed droplet `y=y0+18*t+490*t*t`, clamp/delete at garment.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,329],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":47,"localFrame":0,"set":"Alex signals Nia with old flashlight","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Nia":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":47,"localFrame":329,"set":"Alex signals Nia with old flashlight","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Nia":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_48 — Specialists assess; Alex maintains signal

**Narration:** You keep the light on her window. When it flickers, you tap it again.  Mara stands beside you, speaking to the arriving specialists. The bomb squad examines the wire while firefighters keep the street clear. Nobody asks you to be a hero. They ask you to stay where the girl can see you.  So you do.

## 3. Five planes and environmental dressing
P1 z=-600: layered storm clouds, distant skyline silhouette, water tower, bat-signal haze, rooftop antenna. P2 z=-300: brick seams, fire escape rails, arched windows, gutter pipe, street sign. P3 z=-100: curb stones, parked sedan, road lane marks, utility pole, bus stop post. P4 z=0: puddle reflections, drain grate, ground contact shadows, loose paper. P5 z=150: foreground rain streaks, foreground haze, distant amber windows. Exterior variant: wet reflection opacity .35, rain angle -11deg; drain steam rises. These are art-direction layers; disable elements that contradict this location rather than showing all literal street props indoors.

## 4. 18-node skeletal rig and beat poses
Order/pivot: `pelvis(root); lowerSpine(hip); upperThorax(chest); neckBase(neck); headSkull(neck); clavicleL/R(shoulder socket); shoulderL/R(socket); elbowL/R(elbow); wristL/R(wrist); hipL/R(hip); kneeL/R(knee); ankleL/R+toeBallL/R(ankle/toe)`; origins are rig-local joint pivots, not CSS element centers.

## 8. Cloth and equipment
`cape[n]=sin(frame*.12-n*.35)*3px`, n=0..6, phase delay ~3 frames/node; enable only for Batman. Radio cord node `sin(frame*.15-n*.45)*1.5px` for officer characters; raincoat hood/cuff `sin(frame*.11-n*.4)*1px` only for Nia. Hair sway 0.6px; belt lag 2 frames. Clamp during hard contact.

## 9. Mechanical and shader rigs
`flash=([.55,.3,.8,.45][floor(frame/3)%4])`; after actual tap `flash=1` for 48 frames, then resume; volumetric cone follows prop transform. `fog=safeInterpolate(frame,[0,509],[.30,.82],{easing:E})` only on fogged glass; palm wipe mask subtracts local path only after contact. Bus wire is a fictional visual prop; LED `frame%24<12?1:.25`, no wiring layout. Toy key `rotate(frame*12deg)`, walk while active, contact stop and rebound over 6f, topple 0→90° over 12f; red note mounted to back. Activate only when narrative calls for each element; all others hidden.

## 10. Lighting / post
Key `(x=300,y=120,z=300,color=#7FA4CC,intensity=.80)`; five stylized tones `#202535,#50586B,#A68172,#E9C7A5,#F8E6CF`, rim 3px toward key. Cast floor shadow away from light, opacity .30, blur 8px; `bloom=.12; mist=.15; vignette=.35; grain=.04`. Adapt skin colors to character rather than applying a single palette indiscriminately.

## 11. Principles and audio triggers
Squash/stretch: modest 2% only on impact; anticipation: 4–8f before chosen movement; staging: clear subject; pose-to-pose: `blendPoses(A,B,6)`; follow-through: cloth lag 3f; slow in/out: Bezier E; arcs: quadratic control point `(x+8,y-4)`; secondary: breath; timing: bounded spring; exaggeration: ≤5% pose; solid drawing: joint hierarchy and occlusion; appeal: clear facial silhouette. `SafeSFX` trigger local frames 0 and 18 only when matching actual scene action, gain .10/.12 linear; no generic sound on every beat.

## 12. Entry / exit continuity
`EntryState={"scene":48,"localFrame":0,"set":"Specialists assess; Alex maintains signal","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Specialist":{"root":[1070,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[0,0,1,0],"props":"as narrative specifies"}`

`ExitState={"scene":48,"localFrame":509,"set":"Specialists assess; Alex maintains signal","actors":{"Alex":{"root":[650,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Mara":{"root":[860,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true},"Specialist":{"root":[1070,860,0],"mouth":"CLOSED_M","pose":"neutral","visibility":true}},"camera":[4,0,1,0],"props":"retain any active prop state; resolve against next scene"}`

**Cut handoff:** next scene uses its own explicit entry state; preserve matching props/characters only when story time and set are continuous. Reconcile changes in prop ownership, lighting and positions in edit. Snapshots are proposed blocking, not a verified seamless simulation.

# Scene_49 — Specialist signals safe; Nia reunited

**Narration:** The specialist gives Mara a signal. The device has been made safe. A firefighter opens the door, and the girl climbs down into her mother's arms.  You hear her crying now. So is her mother.  The flashlight goes out in your hand.
